- **Intro:** Breather-Valve Mechanic — In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Cross-Belt Sorter & Telescopic Belt Conveyor Wear Components — Preventive Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) and Zero-Energy State Verification: Voltage verification must read 0 VAC phase-to-phase and phase-to-earth; residual pneumatic pressure after blee…
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage verification must read 0 VAC phase-to-phase and phase-to-earth; residual pneumatic pressure after bleed must be less than 0.05 bar gauge; LOTO tag seal…
- **Standards:** `Act 85`, `SANS 10222-3:2019`, `ISO 12100:2010`, `ISO 13850:2015`
